# Fix visitOver
old = """  protected override visitOver(node: OverNode): void {
    this.append('over(')

    if (node.partitionBy) {
      this.visitNode(node.partitionBy)

      if (node.orderBy) {
        this.append(' ')
      }
    }

    if (node.orderBy) {
      this.visitNode(node.orderBy)
    }

    if (node.frame) {
      this.append(' ')
      this.visitNode(node.frame)
    }

    this.append(')')
  }
"""
new = """  protected override visitOver(node: OverNode): void {
    this.append('over(')

    let hasClause = false

    if (node.partitionBy) {
      this.visitNode(node.partitionBy)
      hasClause = true
    }

    if (node.orderBy) {
      if (hasClause) {
        this.append(' ')
      }

      this.visitNode(node.orderBy)
      hasClause = true
    }

    if (node.frame) {
      if (hasClause) {
        this.append(' ')
      }

      this.visitNode(node.frame)
    }

    this.append(')')
  }
"""
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)

# Fix visitOverFrame: emit 'between ' when end exists
old = """  protected override visitOverFrame(node: OverFrameNode): void {
    this.append(node.mode)
    this.append(' ')
    this.visitNode(node.start)

    if (node.end) {
      this.append(' and ')
      this.visitNode(node.end)
    }
"""
new = """  protected override visitOverFrame(node: OverFrameNode): void {
    this.append(node.mode)
    this.append(' ')

    if (node.end) {
      this.append('between ')
    }

    this.visitNode(node.start)

    if (node.end) {
      this.append(' and ')
      this.visitNode(node.end)
    }
"""
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)

open(p, 'w').write(s)
print('patched')

  protected override visitOver(node: OverNode): void {
    this.append('over(')

    let hasClause = false

    if (node.partitionBy) {
      this.visitNode(node.partitionBy)
      hasClause = true
    }

    if (node.orderBy) {
      if (hasClause) {
        this.append(' ')
      }

      this.visitNode(node.orderBy)
      hasClause = true
    }

    if (node.frame) {
      if (hasClause) {
        this.append(' ')
      }

      this.visitNode(node.frame)
    }

    this.append(')')
  }

  /**
   * Calls the `grouping` function for the column given as the argument.
   *
   * The `grouping` function returns 1 when the column is part of a
   * null-filled super-aggregate row produced by `group by cube`,
   * `group by rollup` or `group by grouping sets`.
   *
   * ### Examples
   *
   * ```ts
   * await db.selectFrom('person')
   *   .select((eb) => [
   *     eb.fn.grouping('person.first_name').as('first_name_grouping'),
   *   ])
   *   .groupByCube('person.first_name', 'person.last_name')
   *   .execute()
   * ```
   *
   * The generated SQL (PostgreSQL):
   *
   * ```sql
   * select grouping("person"."first_name") as "first_name_grouping"
   * from "person"
   * group by cube ("person"."first_name", "person"."last_name")
   * ```
   */
  grouping<RE extends ReferenceExpression<DB, TB>>(
    column: RE,
  ): ExpressionWrapper<DB, TB, number>

  /**
   * Calls the `row_number` window function.
   *
   * ### Examples
   *
   * ```ts
   * await db.selectFrom('person')
   *   .select((eb) =>
   *     eb.fn.rowNumber().over().as('row_number')
   *   )
   *   .execute()
   * ```
   */
  rowNumber(): AggregateFunctionBuilder<DB, TB, number>

  /**
   * Calls the `rank` window function.
   */
  rank(): AggregateFunctionBuilder<DB, TB, number>

  /**
   * Calls the `dense_rank` window function.
   */
  denseRank(): AggregateFunctionBuilder<DB, TB, number>

  /**
   * Calls the `percent_rank` window function.
   */
  percentRank(): AggregateFunctionBuilder<DB, TB, number>

  /**
   * Calls the `cume_dist` window function.
   */
  cumeDist(): AggregateFunctionBuilder<DB, TB, number>

  /**
   * Calls the `ntile` window function.
   *
   * The `buckets` argument is a plain `number` or `bigint` and is emitted
   * as a parameterized query value.
   *
   * ### Examples
   *
   * ```ts
   * await db.selectFrom('person')
   *   .select((eb) =>
   *     eb.fn.ntile(4).over().as('quartile')
   *   )
   *   .execute()
   * ```
   */
  ntile(buckets: number | bigint): AggregateFunctionBuilder<DB, TB, number>

  /**
   * Calls the `first_value` value accessor function.
   *
   * ### Examples

   *   .execute()
   * ```
   */
  firstValue<
    O,
    RE extends ReferenceExpression<DB, TB> = ReferenceExpression<DB, TB>,
  >(expr: RE): AggregateFunctionBuilder<DB, TB, O>

cd /app && cat > /tmp/repro3.js <<'EOF'
const { Kysely, DummyDriver, PostgresAdapter, PostgresIntrospector, PostgresQueryCompiler } = require('/app/dist/cjs/index.js')
const db = new Kysely({ dialect: { createAdapter: () => new PostgresAdapter(), createDriver: () => new DummyDriver(), createIntrospector: (db) => new PostgresIntrospector(db), createQueryCompiler: () => new PostgresQueryCompiler() } })
let pass=0, fail=0
const check=(name, q, exp)=>{const c=q.compile(); const ok=c.sql===exp.sql&&JSON.stringify(c.parameters)===JSON.stringify(exp.parameters); if(ok){pass++; console.log('PASS',name)} else {fail++; console.log('FAIL',name,'\n got',c.sql,c.parameters,'\n exp',exp.sql,exp.parameters)}}

// rows after partition/order
let q = db.selectFrom('person').select((eb) => eb.fn.sum('age').over((ob) => ob.partitionBy('gender').orderBy('age').rows((fb) => fb.betweenPreceding(1).andCurrentRow())).as('s'))
check('rows after partition+order', q, {sql:'select sum("age") over(partition by "gender" order by "age" rows between $1 preceding and current row) as "s" from "person"', parameters:[1]})

// order after rows
q = db.selectFrom('person').select((eb) => eb.fn.sum('age').over((ob) => ob.rows((fb) => fb.unboundedPreceding()).orderBy('age')).as('s'))
check('order after rows', q, {sql:'select sum("age") over(rows unbounded preceding order by "age") as "s" from "person"', parameters:[]})
// note: SQL order of clauses: partition by, order by, frame. Rows before orderBy would compile in given order -> probably invalid but that's user's choice. Let's just check it compiles.

// single-bound following
q = db.selectFrom('person').select((eb) => eb.fn.sum('age').over((ob) => ob.rows((fb) => fb.following(3).excludeCurrentRow())).as('s'))
check('single-bound following exclude current row', q, {sql:'select sum("age") over(rows following $1 exclude current row) as "s" from "person"', parameters:[3]})

// between following and unbounded following

  ['andFollowing', '$1 following', [1]],
  ['andUnboundedFollowing', 'unbounded following', []],
]

  // For non-offset variants, method is called with no arg; but our loop passes 1 to all, which is invalid for andUnboundedPreceding etc. So skip offset methods.
}

const noArg = ['andUnboundedPreceding','andCurrentRow','andUnboundedFollowing']
for (const m of noArg) {

  check('and* '+m, q, {sql:`select sum("age") over(range between unbounded preceding and ${end}) as "s" from "person"`, parameters:[]})
}

  check('and* '+m, q, {sql:`select sum("age") over(range between unbounded preceding and ${end}) as "s" from "person"`, parameters:[1]})

  ['betweenUnboundedPreceding', 'unbounded preceding', null],
  ['betweenPreceding', '$1 preceding', [1]],
  ['betweenCurrentRow', 'current row', null],
  ['betweenFollowing', '$1 following', [1]],

  const params = arg ? [1] : []
  check('starter '+m, q, {sql:`select sum("age") over(rows between ${start} and unbounded following) as "s" from "person"`, parameters:params})

     groupBy: GE,
   ): SelectQueryBuilder<DB, TB, O>
 
+  /**
+   * Adds a `group by cube (...)` clause.
+   *
+   * This is an extension of `group by` supported by some databases like
+   * PostgreSQL and SQL Server.
+   *
+   * ### Examples
+   *
+   * ```ts
+   * await db.selectFrom('person')
+   *   .select(['gender'])
+   *   .groupByCube('gender', 'marital_status')
+   *   .execute()
+   * ```
+   *
+   * The generated SQL (PostgreSQL):
+   *
+   * ```sql
+   * select "gender"
+   * from "person"
+   * group by cube ("gender", "marital_status")
+   * ```
+   */
+  groupByCube<GE extends GroupByArg<DB, TB, O>>(
+    ...columns: GE[]
+  ): SelectQueryBuilder<DB, TB, O>
+
+  /**
+   * Adds a `group by rollup (...)` clause.
+   *
+   * This is an extension of `group by` supported by some databases like
+   * PostgreSQL and SQL Server.
+   *
+   * ### Examples
+   *
+   * ```ts
+   * await db.selectFrom('person')
+   *   .select(['gender'])
+   *   .groupByRollup('gender', 'marital_status')
+   *   .execute()
+   * ```
+   *
+   * The generated SQL (PostgreSQL):
+   *
+   * ```sql
+   * select "gender"
+   * from "person"
+   * group by rollup ("gender", "marital_status")
+   * ```
+   */
+  groupByRollup<GE extends GroupByArg<DB, TB, O>>(
+    ...columns: GE[]
+  ): SelectQueryBuilder<DB, TB, O>
+
+  /**
+   * Adds a `group by grouping sets (...)` clause.
+   *
+   * Each array passed to this method is compiled as its own parenthesized
+   * grouping set.
+   *
+   * ### Examples
+   *
+   * ```ts
+   * await db.selectFrom('person')
+   *   .select(['gender'])
+   *   .groupByGroupingSets(['gender'], ['gender', 'marital_status'])
+   *   .execute()
+   * ```
+   *
+   * The generated SQL (PostgreSQL):
+   *
+   * ```sql
+   * select "gender"
+   * from "person"
+   * group by grouping sets (("gender"), ("gender", "marital_status"))
+   * ```
+   */
+  groupByGroupingSets<GE extends GroupByArg<DB, TB, O>>(
+    ...sets: GE[][]
+  ): SelectQueryBuilder<DB, TB, O>
+
   orderBy<OE extends OrderByExpression<DB, TB, O>>(
     expr: OE,
     modifiers?: OrderByModifiers,
@@ -2422,6 +2508,36 @@ class SelectQueryBuilderImpl<
     })
   }
 
+  groupByCube(...columns: GroupByArg<DB, TB, O>[]): SelectQueryBuilder<DB, TB, O> {
+    return new SelectQueryBuilderImpl({
+      ...this.#props,
+      queryNode: SelectQueryNode.cloneWithGroupByItems(
+        this.#props.queryNode,
+        parseGroupByWithGrouping(columns, 'cube'),
+      ),
+    })
+  }
+
+  groupByRollup(...columns: GroupByArg<DB, TB, O>[]): SelectQueryBuilder<DB, TB, O> {
+    return new SelectQueryBuilderImpl({
+      ...this.#props,
+      queryNode: SelectQueryNode.cloneWithGroupByItems(
+        this.#props.queryNode,
+        parseGroupByWithGrouping(columns, 'rollup'),
+      ),
+    })
+  }
+
+  groupByGroupingSets(...sets: GroupByArg<DB, TB, O>[][]): SelectQueryBuilder<DB, TB, O> {
+    return new SelectQueryBuilderImpl({
+      ...this.#props,
+      queryNode: SelectQueryNode.cloneWithGroupByItems(
+        this.#props.queryNode,
+        parseGroupByGroupingSets(sets),
+      ),
+    })
+  }
+
   limit(
     limit: ValueExpression<DB, TB, number | bigint | null>,
   ): SelectQueryBuilder<DB, TB, O> {

import {
  ColumnType,
  Generated,
  GeneratedAlways,
  JSONColumnType,
} from '../../dist/cjs'

export interface Pet {
  id: Generated<string>
  name: string
  owner_id: number
  species: 'dog' | 'cat'
}

export interface Toy {
  id: Generated<string>
  price: number
  pet_id: string
}

export interface Movie {
  id: Generated<string>
  stars: number
}

export interface Book {
  id: GeneratedAlways<number>
  name: string
}

export interface Database {
  person: Person
  pet: Pet
  movie: Movie
  'some_schema.movie': Movie
  book: Book
  toy: Toy
  person_metadata: PersonMetadata
  action: Action
}

export type Action =
  | {
      id: GeneratedAlways<string>
      type: 'CALL_WEBHOOK'
      queue_id: null
      callback_url: string
    }
  | {
      id: GeneratedAlways<string>
      type: 'DELETE_FROM_QUEUE'
      queue_id: string
      callback_url: null
    }

export interface Person {
  id: Generated<number>
  first_name: string
  last_name: string | null
  age: number
  gender: 'male' | 'female' | 'other'
  marital_status: 'single' | 'married' | 'divorced' | 'widowed' | null
  // A Column that is generated by the DB and which
  // we never want the user to be able to insert or
  // update.
  modified_at: ColumnType<Date, never, never>
  // A column that cannot be inserted, but can be updated.
  deleted_at: ColumnType<Date | null, never, string | undefined>
}

export interface PersonMetadata {
  id: Generated<number>
  person_id: number
  website: JSONColumnType<{ url: string }>
  nicknames: JSONColumnType<string[]>
  profile: JSONColumnType<{
    auth: {
      roles: string[]
      last_login?: { device: string }
    }

import { expectError, expectType } from 'tsd'
import {
  type AggregateFunctionBuilder,
  type ExpressionWrapper,
  type Kysely,
  type OverFrameBuilder,
  type SimplifyFramePlugin,
  sql,
} from '..'
import type { Database } from '../shared'

async function testGroupedAggregation(db: Kysely<Database>) {
  expectType<string>(
    db
      .selectFrom('person')
      .select('first_name')
      .groupByCube('first_name', 'last_name')
      .compile()
      .sql,
  )

  expectType<string>(
    db
      .selectFrom('person')
      .select('first_name')
      .groupByRollup(['first_name', 'last_name'])
      .compile()
      .sql,
  )

  expectType<string>(
    db
      .selectFrom('person')
      .select('first_name')
      .groupByGroupingSets(['first_name'], ['first_name', 'last_name'])
      .compile()
      .sql,
  )

  expectType<string>(
    db
      .selectFrom('person')
      .select('first_name')
      .groupBy('gender')
      .groupByRollup('marital_status')
      .compile()
      .sql,
  )

  expectType<ExpressionWrapper<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn.grouping('first_name'),
  )
}

async function testWindowFunctions(db: Kysely<Database>) {
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn.rowNumber(),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn.rank(),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn.denseRank(),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn.percentRank(),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn.cumeDist(),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn.ntile(4),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn.ntile(4n),
  )

  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', string | null>>(
    db.fn.firstValue<string | null>('last_name'),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', string | null>>(
    db.fn.lastValue<string | null>('last_name'),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', string | null>>(
    db.fn.nthValue<string | null>('last_name', 2),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', string | null>>(
    db.fn.lag<string | null>('last_name', 1, 'unknown'),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', string | null>>(
    db.fn.lead<string | null>('last_name', 1),
  )

  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', string | null>>(
    db.fn.firstValue<string | null>('last_name').respectNulls(),
  )
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', string | null>>(
    db.fn.firstValue<string | null>('last_name').ignoreNulls(),
  )

  expectError(db.fn.ntile(sql.lit(1)))
  expectError(db.fn.lag<number>('age', sql.lit(1)))
}

async function testWindowFrames(db: Kysely<Database>) {
  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn
      .rowNumber()
      .over((ob) =>
        ob
          .partitionBy('gender')
          .orderBy('age')
          .rows((fb) =>
            fb.betweenUnboundedPreceding().andCurrentRow().excludeTies(),
          ),
      ),
  )

  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn.rowNumber().over((ob) =>
      ob.range((fb) => fb.betweenPreceding(1).andFollowing(2).excludeGroup()),
    ),
  )

  expectType<AggregateFunctionBuilder<Database, 'person', number>>(
    db.fn.rowNumber().over((ob) =>
      ob.groups((fb) => fb.unboundedPreceding().excludeNoOthers()),
    ),
  )

  expectType<OverFrameBuilder<Database, 'person'>>(
    // @ts-expect-error The callback builder is not assignable to the result type.
    (() => undefined) as any,
  )

  expectType<SimplifyFramePlugin>(new SimplifyFramePlugin())
}

  db.selectFrom('person').select((eb) => {

      ),

+  /**
+   * Adds a `respect nulls` clause after the function's arguments.
+   *
+   * This is only applicable to value accessor functions such as
+   * `first_value`, `last_value`, `nth_value`, `lag` and `lead`.
+   *
+   * ### Examples
+   *
+   * ```ts
+   * const result = await db
+   *   .selectFrom('person')
+   *   .select((eb) =>
+   *     eb.fn.firstValue('first_name').respectNulls().over().as('first_first_name')
+   *   )
+   *   .execute()
+   * ```
+   *
+   * The generated SQL (PostgreSQL):
+   *
+   * ```sql
+   * select first_value("first_name") respect nulls over() as "first_first_name"
+   * from "person"
+   * ```
+   */
+  respectNulls(): AggregateFunctionBuilder<DB, TB, O> {
+    return new AggregateFunctionBuilder({
+      ...this.#props,
+      aggregateFunctionNode: AggregateFunctionNode.cloneWithNulls(
+        this.#props.aggregateFunctionNode,
+        'respect nulls',
+      ),
+    })
+  }
+

+   * Adds an `ignore nulls` clause after the function's arguments.
+   *
+   * This is only applicable to value accessor functions such as
+   * `first_value`, `last_value`, `nth_value`, `lag` and `lead`.
+   *
+   * ### Examples
+   *
+   * ```ts
+   * const result = await db
+   *   .selectFrom('person')
+   *   .select((eb) =>
+   *     eb.fn.firstValue('first_name').ignoreNulls().over().as('first_first_name')
+   *   )
+   *   .execute()
+   * ```
+   *
+   * The generated SQL (PostgreSQL):
+   *
+   * ```sql
+   * select first_value("first_name") ignore nulls over() as "first_first_name"
+   * from "person"
+   * ```
+   */
+  ignoreNulls(): AggregateFunctionBuilder<DB, TB, O> {
+    return new AggregateFunctionBuilder({
+      ...this.#props,
+      aggregateFunctionNode: AggregateFunctionNode.cloneWithNulls(
+        this.#props.aggregateFunctionNode,
+        'ignore nulls',
+      ),
+    })
+  }

import type { Expression } from '../expression/expression.js'
import { OverBoundNode } from '../operation-node/over-bound-node.js'
import { OverExclusionNode } from '../operation-node/over-exclusion-node.js'
import {
  OverFrameNode,
  type OverFrameMode,
} from '../operation-node/over-frame-node.js'
import type { OperationNodeSource } from '../operation-node/operation-node-source.js'
import { parseValueExpression } from '../parser/value-parser.js'
import { freeze } from '../util/object-utils.js'

export type OverOffset = number | bigint | Expression<any>

export interface OverFrameBuilderProps {
  readonly mode: OverFrameMode
  readonly overFrameNode?: OverFrameNode
}

/**
 * A builder for the `rows`, `range` or `groups` frame of an `over` clause.
 */
export class OverFrameBuilder<DB, TB extends keyof DB> {
  readonly #props: OverFrameBuilderProps

  constructor(props: OverFrameBuilderProps) {
    this.#props = freeze(props)
  }

  unboundedPreceding(): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameExclusionBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.create(
        this.#props.mode,
        OverBoundNode.create('unbounded preceding'),
      ),
    })
  }

  preceding(offset: OverOffset): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameExclusionBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.create(
        this.#props.mode,
        OverBoundNode.create('preceding', parseValueExpression(offset)),
      ),
    })
  }

  currentRow(): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameExclusionBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.create(
        this.#props.mode,
        OverBoundNode.create('current row'),
      ),
    })
  }

  following(offset: OverOffset): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameExclusionBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.create(
        this.#props.mode,
        OverBoundNode.create('following', parseValueExpression(offset)),
      ),
    })
  }

  unboundedFollowing(): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameExclusionBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.create(
        this.#props.mode,
        OverBoundNode.create('unbounded following'),
      ),
    })
  }

  betweenUnboundedPreceding(): OverFrameTwoSidedStartBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameTwoSidedStartBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.create(
        this.#props.mode,
        OverBoundNode.create('unbounded preceding'),
      ),
    })
  }

  betweenPreceding(offset: OverOffset): OverFrameTwoSidedStartBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameTwoSidedStartBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.create(
        this.#props.mode,
        OverBoundNode.create('preceding', parseValueExpression(offset)),
      ),
    })
  }

  betweenCurrentRow(): OverFrameTwoSidedStartBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameTwoSidedStartBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.create(
        this.#props.mode,
        OverBoundNode.create('current row'),
      ),
    })
  }

  betweenFollowing(offset: OverOffset): OverFrameTwoSidedStartBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameTwoSidedStartBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.create(
        this.#props.mode,
        OverBoundNode.create('following', parseValueExpression(offset)),
      ),
    })
  }
}

/**
 * A builder for the start bound of a two-sided window frame. The frame
 * must be completed with one of the `and*` methods.
 */
export class OverFrameTwoSidedStartBuilder<DB, TB extends keyof DB> {
  readonly #props: OverFrameBuilderProps

  constructor(props: OverFrameBuilderProps) {
    this.#props = freeze(props)
  }

  andUnboundedPreceding(): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return this.#withEnd(OverBoundNode.create('unbounded preceding'))
  }

  andPreceding(offset: OverOffset): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return this.#withEnd(
      OverBoundNode.create('preceding', parseValueExpression(offset)),
    )
  }

  andCurrentRow(): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return this.#withEnd(OverBoundNode.create('current row'))
  }

  andFollowing(offset: OverOffset): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return this.#withEnd(
      OverBoundNode.create('following', parseValueExpression(offset)),
    )
  }

  andUnboundedFollowing(): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return this.#withEnd(OverBoundNode.create('unbounded following'))
  }

  #withEnd(end: OverBoundNode): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameExclusionBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.cloneWithEnd(
        this.#props.overFrameNode!,
        end,
      ),
    })
  }
}

/**
 * A builder for a complete window frame that can optionally be augmented
 * with an exclusion clause.
 */
export class OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB extends keyof DB>
  implements OperationNodeSource
{
  readonly #props: OverFrameBuilderProps

  constructor(props: OverFrameBuilderProps) {
    this.#props = freeze(props)
  }

  excludeCurrentRow(): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return this.#withExclusion(OverExclusionNode.create('current row'))
  }

  excludeGroup(): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return this.#withExclusion(OverExclusionNode.create('group'))
  }

  excludeTies(): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return this.#withExclusion(OverExclusionNode.create('ties'))
  }

  excludeNoOthers(): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return this.#withExclusion(OverExclusionNode.create('no others'))
  }

  #withExclusion(exclusion: OverExclusionNode): OverFrameExclusionBuilder<DB, TB> {
    return new OverFrameExclusionBuilder({
      ...this.#props,
      overFrameNode: OverFrameNode.cloneWithExclusion(
        this.#props.overFrameNode!,
        exclusion,
      ),
    })
  }

  toOperationNode(): OverFrameNode {
    return this.#props.overFrameNode!
  }
}


STEP 113 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat src/plugin/simplify-frame/simplify-frame-transformer.ts src/plugin/simplify-frame/simplify-frame-plugin.ts
OUTPUT:
import { OperationNodeTransformer } from '../../operation-node/operation-node-transformer.js'
import type { OverBoundNode } from '../../operation-node/over-bound-node.js'
import type { OverNode } from '../../operation-node/over-node.js'

/**
 * @internal
 */
export class SimplifyFrameTransformer extends OperationNodeTransformer {
  protected override transformOver(node: OverNode): OverNode {
    if (node.frame && isRedundantFrame(node)) {
      return super.transformOver({
        ...node,
        frame: undefined,
      })
    }

    return super.transformOver(node)
  }
}

function isRedundantFrame(node: OverNode): boolean {
  const frame = node.frame!

  if (frame.mode !== 'range' || frame.exclusion) {
    return false
  }

  const start = frame.start
  const end = frame.end

  if (node.orderBy) {
    // The SQL-standard implicit default is:
    // `range between unbounded preceding and current row`.
    // A single-bound `unbounded preceding` frame is shorthand for the same.
    if (end) {
      return (
        isBound(start, 'unbounded preceding') &&
        isBound(end, 'current row')
      )
    }

    return isBound(start, 'unbounded preceding')
  }

  // Without `order by`, the SQL-standard implicit default is:
  // `range between unbounded preceding and unbounded following`.
  return (
    end !== undefined &&
    isBound(start, 'unbounded preceding') &&
    isBound(end, 'unbounded following')
  )
}

function isBound(node: OverBoundNode, type: OverBoundNode['type']): boolean {
  return node.type === type && node.value === undefined
}
import type { QueryResult } from '../../driver/database-connection.js'
import type { RootOperationNode } from '../../query-compiler/query-compiler.js'
import type { UnknownRow } from '../../util/type-utils.js'
import type {
  KyselyPlugin,
  PluginTransformQueryArgs,
  PluginTransformResultArgs,
} from '../kysely-plugin.js'
import { SimplifyFrameTransformer } from './simplify-frame-transformer.js'

/**
 * Plugin that removes redundant window frame clauses from `over` calls.
 *
 * When an `over` clause contains an `order by`, the database implicitly
 * applies `range between unbounded preceding and current row`. When it
 * doesn't contain an `order by`, the implicit default is
 * `range between unbounded preceding and unbounded following`.
 *
 * This plugin detects frames that replicate these implicit defaults and
 * strips them before compilation. Frames that use `rows` or `groups` mode,
 * carry an exclusion clause, or use non-default bound types are preserved.
 *
 * ### Examples
 *
 * ```ts
 * import { SimplifyFramePlugin } from 'kysely'
 *
 * const db = new Kysely({
 *   dialect,
 *   plugins: [new SimplifyFramePlugin()],
 * })
 *
 * // The `range between unbounded preceding and current row` clause is
 * // redundant because it's already the implicit default when `order by`
 * // is present, so it is stripped:
 * await db.selectFrom('person')
 *   .select((eb) => eb.fn.sum('age')
 *     .over((ob) => ob.orderBy('first_name').range((fb) => fb.betweenUnboundedPreceding().andCurrentRow()))
 *     .as('total_age'))
 *   .execute()
 * ```
 */
export class SimplifyFramePlugin implements KyselyPlugin {
  readonly #transformer = new SimplifyFrameTransformer()

  transformQuery(args: PluginTransformQueryArgs): RootOperationNode {
    return this.#transformer.transformNode(args.node, args.queryId)
  }

  transformResult(
    args: PluginTransformResultArgs,
  ): Promise<QueryResult<UnknownRow>> {
    return Promise.resolve(args.result)
  }
}
